### Smallest proposed implementation: one additive ephemeral capture delta

Only modify a **copy in memory of the existing CI supervisor**, with routine evidence in this design and `docs/runbooks/phase-q-acceptance.md`. No repository executable, test body/helper, package, config, registry, schema, Harness pin/validator, cache, workflow runtime or acceptance command changes. The original qualified source remains available unchanged. No separate profiling program, output archive, new child process/IPC, custom reporter, OS-process-tree probe, filesystem probe, provider call or project-state access is added.

The delta has two purposes, using the **same native `pnpm ci:check` output** and existing stopwatch:

- **Freeze the completion boundary before any kill/drain.** Add detached booleans/enums for principal `HasExited` (or unavailable), each stream's existing EOF flag, each pending read's pending/completed/faulted/cancelled/absent state and whether an incomplete line is held. Preserve already-parsed file/test summary `seen` states and supported counts; unsupported/duplicate summary remains unsupported, not invented zero. Reading these facts is point-in-time observation, not an atomic process-tree/cleanup assertion. Never use them to move the deadline, wait longer, change exit authority or override the original first supervision reason.
- **Retain bounded ordinary file progress instead of discarding it.** After the existing raw bounds checks and stage/version/rendering admission, recognize only exact installed ordinary file rows for a fresh pinned map of the current **151 public test paths** and source hashes. Keep a fixed completion bitmap, count of admitted known file reports/gaps, first failed-shaped file ID, two slowest duration-bearing file reports, and last file receipt. Retain numeric IDs/counts/rounded durations only; no test title, parameter, assertion body, raw error, private path or output tail. Missing duration is null, not zero. Use existing elapsed observations for last input/file receipt; bounded consumed-character/read counters describe transport volume, **not reader throughput, CPU attribution or proof of backpressure**. All pre-existing summary/diagnostic/stage handling still receives its original input first.

Bind IDs to a fresh ordered public map; the previously observed path-map identity was **151 / `07CCDBEC575A5B43DB90FEA29D53AC201D564DE7EC61BABD0E439F4A79B880B1`**, but its old source hashes must not be reused after the fixture repair. Paths absent from the admitted map, unknown banners/labels/suffixes, duplicate completions, ambiguous framing, arithmetic/clock faults or partial EOF produce explicit observation gaps, never fallback parsing or execution permission. A bitmap's unset IDs are **not observed completed**, not proof those files were collected, are currently running or are stuck. Reported durations overlap and cannot be subtracted as exclusive CPU/idle time.

Keep the existing **64-KiB stream, 8-KiB line, 1024-byte diagnostic and 2048-byte terminal-record bounds**, incremental discard, original **900-second predicate**, **5-second exit / 3-second drain paths**, native arguments, sanitized environment/PATHEXT, read buffers/wait ordering, two workers and every test/hook timeout unchanged. Target the new compact snapshot at **at most 640 UTF-8 bytes**, inside the existing record/state envelope; prove the joint worst case rather than enlarging any budget. If optional metadata cannot fit, suppress only that addition with a small explicit unavailable marker; preserve the original record/result/reason. No raw-check bypass, new termination condition, cleanup eligibility or longer wait is permitted. Observation availability does not replace native exit/closed-stream authority or redefine CI success.

### Qualification and one-run boundary, both future approvals

**First approval: memory-only implementation and synthetic qualification only.** Preserve and replay the existing **189 baseline/stage checks against the immutable qualified source**, unchanged, then test the additive copy with a finite focused delta matrix. Do not rewrite old hash/guard expectations to make the modified source pass. Use fake process/readers/clocks and literal installed-rendering samples: active/exited principal with open/closed/late pipes; pre-kill versus post-drain state; supported/missing/failed/duplicate summaries; native 0/nonzero/no exit; chunk splits, Unicode/ANSI/CRLF/partial EOF; known/foreign/failed/skipped/todo module rows; missing/zero/rounded durations; duplicate/missing IDs; ranking/bitmap bounds; late input after freeze; clock/arithmetic failures; privacy canaries; high-volume discard; unchanged effect/guard trace and native outcome; maximal original record plus new metadata inside all original limits. Check literal reversible scope/source integrity and static syntax. Fakes must not launch a process, touch a filesystem or load real test fixtures. Stop at the first failed/unknown check; no automatic repair/retry or real CI follows qualification.

| Observed result at the unchanged boundary | Bounded follow-up decision, not a diagnosis or implementation grant |
| --- | --- |
| Principal already exited, one or both streams not closed | Inspect the existing launcher/stdio ownership/descendant-handle path, rather than assuming slow tests. Pre-kill principal exit is not all-writer cleanup. |
| Principal active, recognizable final test summary received, no next CI stage observed | Inspect post-summary runner/worker shutdown or command-chain completion. A summary is not proof Vitest/pnpm exited or that an assertion passed. |
| Principal active, partial known file completion and no final summary | Use the fresh public file IDs/durations and coverage gaps to choose one concrete fixture/import/dependency-isolation review; do not assume an unset/last ID is an active hung file. Cached rankings are only supporting historical hints. |
| Native nonzero exit and closed streams | Stop with the native failure and retained public failed-file hint/summary availability; review that actual failure before any repair. |
| Metadata unsupported/unavailable | Report the precise observation gap and stop investigation at its approved boundary; no reporter switch, new probe ladder or acceptance waiver follows automatically. |
| Native CI exit 0 with closed streams | Under explicit conditional authority, continue audit -> Q smoke -> general smoke, with the same per-command ceiling and cleanup requirements. Do not require a duplicate CI or replay the original gate. |
